  "content": "//! Multi-threaded zerocopy runtime — message-moving\n//! infrastructure.\n//!\n//! Pairs with [`crate::actor_manager`], which owns the actor\n//! model surface ([`ActorZC`], [`ContextZC`], [`ActorManager`]).\n//! This module is one consumer of that surface; future\n//! transports (M:N scheduler, alternative channel types, …)\n//! would be peers.\n//!\n//! Responsibilities held here:\n//!\n//! - Owns the [`Pool<S>`]; spawns actor threads; wires\n//!   per-actor `mpsc` channels; runs the warmup / measure /\n//!   shutdown lifecycle.\n//! - Knows nothing about \"seeds\", named lookups, or what a\n//!   benchmark is. Initial messages are passed to\n//!   [`RuntimeZC::run`] directly by the app; the runtime\n//!   delivers them after spawn / before warmup, then forgets.\n//! - Drains the manager via [`ActorManager::take_actors`] +\n//!   [`ActorManager::probe_name_prefix`] when `run` is called.\n//!\n//! Two run entry points on [`RuntimeZC`]:\n//!\n//! - [`RuntimeZC::run`] — `tprobe`-instrumented; returns\n//!   `(count, TProbe)` per actor. Used by the `goalzc` binary.\n//! - [`RuntimeZC::run_no_probe`] — probe-free; returns `count`\n//!   per actor. Used by the `goalzc-crit` criterion bench so\n//!   measurement is not contaminated by probe overhead.\n//!\n//! Lifecycle inside `run` / `run_no_probe`:\n//!\n//! - Drain actors from the Manager.\n//! - Build per-actor channels.\n//! - Spawn one thread per actor, optionally pinned.\n//! - Deliver `initial_messages` over the senders.\n//! - Sleep `warmup`.\n//! - Send `ClearProbe` to every actor.\n//! - Sleep `measurement`.\n//! - Send `Shutdown` to every actor.\n//! - Drop main-thread senders so the channels close.\n//! - Join, return per-actor results.\n//!\n//! Drain invariant: by the time the join completes, every\n//! [`PooledMsg`] that was ever in flight has been dropped, so\n//! `pool.free_len() == pool.size()`.\n\nuse std::sync::mpsc::{self, Receiver, Sender};\nuse std::thread::{self, JoinHandle};\nuse std::time::Duration;\n\nuse crate::actor_manager::{ActorManager, ActorZC, ContextZC};\nuse crate::pool::{BufRefStore, MutexLifo, Pool, PoolError, PooledMsg};\nuse tprobe::TProbe;\n\n/// Internal channel payload.\n///\n/// - `User(msg)` — caller-visible traffic.\n/// - `ClearProbe` — runtime injects at warmup → measurement\n///   boundary so the report only sees steady-state data.\n/// - `Shutdown` — runtime injects after the measurement\n///   window; actor exits its `recv` loop on receipt.\nenum SignalZC<S: BufRefStore> {\n    User(PooledMsg<S>),\n    ClearProbe,\n    Shutdown,\n}\n\n/// Multi-threaded zerocopy runtime — message-moving\n/// infrastructure.\n///\n/// - Owns the [`Pool<S>`] (cloned cheaply to each actor\n///   thread).\n/// - Spawns threads, wires channels, runs the warmup /\n///   measure / shutdown lifecycle.\n/// - Knows nothing about \"seeds\", named lookups, or what's\n///   being measured. Initial messages come in as a parameter\n///   to `run` and are delivered after spawn / before warmup.\n/// - Generic over the store backend `S` so concurrency\n///   strategies can be swapped and benchmarked. Default\n///   [`MutexLifo`].\npub struct RuntimeZC<S: BufRefStore = MutexLifo> {\n    pool: Pool<S>,\n}\n\nimpl<S: BufRefStore + 'static> RuntimeZC<S> {\n    /// Build a runtime that draws buffers from `pool`.\n    ///\n    /// - The runtime stores a cheap `Arc` clone; the caller\n    ///   keeps the original handle so it can build initial\n    ///   messages or inspect (`free_len`, `size`).\n    pub fn new(pool: Pool<S>) -> Self {\n        Self { pool }\n    }\n\n    /// Borrow the runtime's pool handle.\n    pub fn pool(&self) -> &Pool<S> {\n        &self.pool\n    }\n\n    /// Drain the manager's actors, run with `tprobe`\n    /// instrumentation.\n    ///\n    /// - Each actor thread owns a [`TProbe`] recording per-\n    ///   `handle_message` latency at `inner=1`.\n    /// - `initial_messages` are delivered after thread spawn,\n    ///   before warmup.\n    /// - Returns `(count, probe)` per actor in id order.\n    pub fn run(\n        &mut self,\n        mgr: &mut ActorManager<S>,\n        initial_messages: Vec<(u32, PooledMsg<S>)>,\n        warmup: Duration,\n        measurement: Duration,\n        pin_cores: &[usize],\n    ) -> Vec<(u64, TProbe)> {\n        self.run_inner(\n            mgr,\n            initial_messages,\n            warmup,\n            measurement,\n            pin_cores,\n            actor_loop_probe::<S>,\n        )\n    }\n\n    /// Same orchestration as [`RuntimeZC::run`], minus the\n    /// per-message `TProbe` calls in the hot loop.\n    ///\n    /// - Used by the `goalzc-crit` criterion bench so the\n    ///   measurement is probe-clean.\n    /// - The lesson learned from `goal2-crit`'s probe\n    ///   contamination: keeping a probe-free path on the\n    ///   runtime from the start avoids the trap of a bench\n    ///   that secretly measures `work + tprobe`.\n    pub fn run_no_probe(\n        &mut self,\n        mgr: &mut ActorManager<S>,\n        initial_messages: Vec<(u32, PooledMsg<S>)>,\n        warmup: Duration,\n        measurement: Duration,\n        pin_cores: &[usize],\n    ) -> Vec<u64> {\n        self.run_inner(\n            mgr,\n            initial_messages,\n            warmup,\n            measurement,\n            pin_cores,\n            actor_loop_no_probe::<S>,\n        )\n    }\n\n    /// Shared orchestration body.\n    ///\n    /// - Drains the manager, builds channels, spawns one\n    ///   thread per actor, delivers initial messages, runs\n    ///   the warmup / measure / shutdown lifecycle, joins,\n    ///   and returns per-actor results.\n    /// - Per-thread work is delegated to `actor_loop`, a\n    ///   function pointer (`ActorLoopFn<S, R>`) so this method\n    ///   monomorphizes per `R` without the closure / `Fn`-bound\n    ///   overhead.\n    fn run_inner<R: Send + 'static>(\n        &mut self,\n        mgr: &mut ActorManager<S>,\n        initial_messages: Vec<(u32, PooledMsg<S>)>,\n        warmup: Duration,\n        measurement: Duration,\n        pin_cores: &[usize],\n        actor_loop: ActorLoopFn<S, R>,\n    ) -> Vec<R> {\n        let actors = mgr.take_actors();\n        let n = actors.len();\n        let pool = self.pool.clone();\n        let probe_name_prefix = mgr.probe_name_prefix().to_string();\n\n        let mut senders: Vec<Sender<SignalZC<S>>> = Vec::with_capacity(n);\n        let mut receivers: Vec<Option<Receiver<SignalZC<S>>>> = Vec::with_capacity(n);\n        for _ in 0..n {\n            let (tx, rx) = mpsc::channel();\n            senders.push(tx);\n            receivers.push(Some(rx));\n        }\n\n        let mut handles: Vec<JoinHandle<R>> = Vec::with_capacity(n);\n        for (id, actor) in actors.into_iter().enumerate() {\n            #[allow(clippy::unwrap_used)]\n            // OK: receivers[id] was set to `Some(...)` in the loop above.\n            let rx = receivers[id].take().unwrap();\n            let peers: Vec<Sender<SignalZC<S>>> = senders.to_vec();\n            let probe_name = format!(\"{}.actor{}\", probe_name_prefix, id);\n            let pin_target = if pin_cores.is_empty() {\n                None\n            } else {\n                Some(pin_cores[id % pin_cores.len()])\n            };\n            let actor_pool = pool.clone();\n            let h = thread::Builder::new()\n                .name(format!(\"actor-zc-{id}\"))\n                .spawn(move || {\n                    tprobe::pin::pin_current(pin_target);\n                    actor_loop(actor, rx, peers, actor_pool, probe_name)\n                })\n                .expect(\"failed to spawn actor thread\");\n            handles.push(h);\n        }\n\n        for (dst, msg) in initial_messages {\n            #[allow(clippy::unwrap_used)]\n            // OK: receivers are alive (threads just spawned); a send\n            //   failure indicates an immediate thread panic at startup\n            //   which the join below surfaces with a clearer message.\n            senders[dst as usize].send(SignalZC::User(msg)).unwrap();\n        }\n\n        thread::sleep(warmup);\n        for tx in &senders {\n            let _ = tx.send(SignalZC::ClearProbe);\n        }\n\n        thread::sleep(measurement);\n        for tx in &senders {\n            let _ = tx.send(SignalZC::Shutdown);\n        }\n        drop(senders);\n\n        let mut results = Vec::with_capacity(n);\n        for h in handles {\n            results.push(h.join().expect(\"actor thread panicked\"));\n        }\n        results\n    }\n}\n\n/// Function-pointer type for the per-thread actor loop, used\n/// by [`RuntimeZC::run_inner`]. `fn(...) -> R` is `Copy +\n/// Send + Sync`, which keeps the orchestration scaffold\n/// generic without closure / `Fn`-bound complications.\ntype ActorLoopFn<S, R> = fn(\n    Box<dyn ActorZC<S> + Send>,\n    Receiver<SignalZC<S>>,\n    Vec<Sender<SignalZC<S>>>,\n    Pool<S>,\n    String,\n) -> R;\n\n/// `ContextZC` impl used inside each actor thread.\n///\n/// - Holds borrowed references to the actor's peer senders\n///   and the shared pool.\n/// - `get_msg` delegates to [`Pool::get_msg`].\n/// - `send` ships a `PooledMsg` over the appropriate channel;\n///   on closed channel the message drops back to the pool.\nstruct MultiCtxZC<'a, S: BufRefStore> {\n    senders: &'a [Sender<SignalZC<S>>],\n    pool: &'a Pool<S>,\n}\n\nimpl<S: BufRefStore> ContextZC<S> for MultiCtxZC<'_, S> {\n    fn get_msg(&mut self, size: usize) -> Result<PooledMsg<S>, PoolError> {\n        self.pool.get_msg(size)\n    }\n\n    fn send(&mut self, dst_id: u32, msg: PooledMsg<S>) {\n        // Ignored Err contains the un-sent SignalZC::User(msg);\n        // dropping it returns the buffer to the pool.\n        let _ = self.senders[dst_id as usize].send(SignalZC::User(msg));\n    }\n}\n\n/// Per-thread loop with `tprobe` instrumentation.\n///\n/// - One scope per `handle_message` (`inner=1`).\n/// - `Signal::User` records.\n/// - `Signal::ClearProbe` resets probe + count.\n/// - `Signal::Shutdown` (or closed channel) exits.\nfn actor_loop_probe<S: BufRefStore + 'static>(\n    mut actor: Box<dyn ActorZC<S> + Send>,\n    rx: Receiver<SignalZC<S>>,\n    peers: Vec<Sender<SignalZC<S>>>,\n    pool: Pool<S>,\n    probe_name: String,\n) -> (u64, TProbe) {\n    let mut probe = TProbe::new(&probe_name);\n    let mut count: u64 = 0;\n    loop {\n        match rx.recv() {\n            Ok(SignalZC::User(msg)) => {\n                let id = probe.start(0);\n                let mut ctx = MultiCtxZC {\n                    senders: &peers,\n                    pool: &pool,\n                };\n                actor.handle_message(&mut ctx, &msg);\n                probe.end(id);\n                drop(msg);\n                count += 1;\n            }\n            Ok(SignalZC::ClearProbe) => {\n                probe.clear();\n                count = 0;\n            }\n            Ok(SignalZC::Shutdown) | Err(_) => break,\n        }\n    }\n    (count, probe)\n}\n\n/// Per-thread loop with no probe involvement.\n///\n/// - Identical to [`actor_loop_probe`] minus `probe.start` /\n///   `probe.end` / `probe.clear`.\n/// - `_probe_name` is unused but kept in the signature so the\n///   function pointer matches [`ActorLoopFn`] without\n///   special-casing.\nfn actor_loop_no_probe<S: BufRefStore + 'static>(\n    mut actor: Box<dyn ActorZC<S> + Send>,\n    rx: Receiver<SignalZC<S>>,\n    peers: Vec<Sender<SignalZC<S>>>,\n    pool: Pool<S>,\n    _probe_name: String,\n) -> u64 {\n    let mut count: u64 = 0;\n    loop {\n        match rx.recv() {\n            Ok(SignalZC::User(msg)) => {\n                let mut ctx = MultiCtxZC {\n                    senders: &peers,\n                    pool: &pool,\n                };\n                actor.handle_message(&mut ctx, &msg);\n                drop(msg);\n                count += 1;\n            }\n            Ok(SignalZC::ClearProbe) => {\n                count = 0;\n            }\n            Ok(SignalZC::Shutdown) | Err(_) => break,\n        }\n    }\n    count\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use crate::pool::Pool;\n\n    /// Generic ping-pong actor reusable across `S` choices.\n    /// On each message: get a same-sized reply buffer, send\n    /// to the configured peer, drop the inbound `PooledMsg`\n    /// (returns its buffer to the pool).\n    struct PingPong {\n        peer: u32,\n    }\n\n    impl<S: BufRefStore> ActorZC<S> for PingPong {\n        fn handle_message(&mut self, ctx: &mut dyn ContextZC<S>, msg: &[u8]) {\n            let reply = ctx.get_msg(msg.len()).expect(\"get_msg should succeed\");\n            ctx.send(self.peer, reply);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Two-actor ping-pong drives traffic and shuts down\n    /// cleanly; both actors record nonzero counts.\n    #[test]\n    fn ping_pong_runs_and_shuts_down() {\n        let pool: Pool = Pool::new(64, 4);\n        let mut rt = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());\n        let mut mgr = ActorManager::new(\"test.zc\");\n        let a = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 1 });\n        let _b = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 0 });\n        let initial = vec![(a, pool.get_msg(8).expect(\"seed\"))];\n        let results = rt.run(\n            &mut mgr,\n            initial,\n            Duration::from_millis(40),\n            Duration::from_millis(40),\n            &[],\n        );\n        assert_eq!(results.len(), 2);\n        let total: u64 = results.iter().map(|(c, _)| *c).sum();\n        assert!(total > 0, \"expected nonzero throughput, got count={total}\");\n    }\n\n    /// After `run` returns, every buffer the pool ever\n    /// handed out has come back. Tests the drain story\n    /// across the channel-close path.\n    #[test]\n    fn pool_is_full_after_shutdown() {\n        let pool: Pool = Pool::new(64, 4);\n        let mut rt = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());\n        let mut mgr = ActorManager::new(\"test.drain\");\n        let a = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 1 });\n        let _b = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 0 });\n        let initial = vec![(a, pool.get_msg(8).expect(\"seed\"))];\n        let _ = rt.run(\n            &mut mgr,\n            initial,\n            Duration::from_millis(20),\n            Duration::from_millis(20),\n            &[],\n        );\n        assert_eq!(\n            pool.free_len(),\n            pool.size(),\n            \"all buffers should return to pool after shutdown\"\n        );\n    }\n\n    /// `run_no_probe` orchestrates identically and returns\n    /// per-actor counts (no probes). Validates that the\n    /// probe-clean path runs the same workload.\n    #[test]\n    fn run_no_probe_returns_counts() {\n        let pool: Pool = Pool::new(64, 4);\n        let mut rt = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());\n        let mut mgr = ActorManager::new(\"test.np\");\n        let a = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 1 });\n        let _b = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 0 });\n        let initial = vec![(a, pool.get_msg(8).expect(\"seed\"))];\n        let results = rt.run_no_probe(\n            &mut mgr,\n            initial,\n            Duration::from_millis(20),\n            Duration::from_millis(20),\n            &[],\n        );\n        assert_eq!(results.len(), 2);\n        let total: u64 = results.iter().sum();\n        assert!(total > 0, \"expected nonzero throughput, got {total}\");\n        assert_eq!(\n            pool.free_len(),\n            pool.size(),\n            \"all buffers should return to pool after no-probe shutdown\"\n        );\n    }\n\n    /// `Runtime::pool` exposes the caller's handle; the\n    /// caller can build initial messages from it without\n    /// holding a separate clone.\n    #[test]\n    fn runtime_pool_accessor_returns_same_handle() {\n        let pool: Pool = Pool::new(32, 2);\n        let rt = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());\n        assert_eq!(rt.pool().msg_size(), 32);\n        assert_eq!(rt.pool().size(), 2);\n    }\n}\n"
}

  "old_string": "## ActorManager + RuntimeZC: split catalog from transport (0.5.0-2)\n\nAdds the multi-threaded zerocopy actor runtime — `ActorZC` /\n`ContextZC` traits, an `ActorManager<S>` catalog, a `RuntimeZC<S>`\ntransport, with `run` and `run_no_probe` entry points. The shipped\nshape deviates from the `0.5.0-0` plan in three ways, all decided\nduring review:\n\n- Two types instead of one — `ActorManager<S>` (catalog) + `RuntimeZC<S>` (transport). The runtime moves messages; the manager holds instances and assigns ids; they only touch through `take_actors` + `probe_name_prefix` when `run` is called.\n- Direct instance registration rather than factory closures. `ActorZC: Send` is on the trait, so the factory pattern's \"don't require Send\" benefit is moot; `mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 1 })` reads cleaner than `mgr.add_actor(|| PingPong { peer: 1 })`.\n- Initial messages live in the app, not the runtime. `run(&mut mgr, initial_messages, …)` takes them as a parameter; neither manager nor runtime stores them. The benchmark / app is what knows \"ping-pong needs one seed message\".\n\n### Tests added (6 new in `runtime_zc::tests`, 28 total in lib)\n\n- `ping_pong_runs_and_shuts_down` — two-actor ping-pong drives traffic for 40 ms each phase; both actors record nonzero counts.\n- `pool_is_full_after_shutdown` — after `run` returns, every buffer that was ever in flight has come back; `pool.free_len() == pool.size()`.\n- `run_no_probe_returns_counts` — probe-clean path runs the same workload, returns counts only, drains the pool the same way.\n- `take_actors_drains_and_resets` — after `take_actors`, the manager is empty and follow-up `add` restarts ids from 0.\n- `runtime_pool_accessor_returns_same_handle` — `RuntimeZC::pool()` exposes the caller's handle (verifies `msg_size` / `size`).\n- `manager_exposes_probe_name_prefix` — verifies the prefix accessor; full `\"<prefix>.actor<id>\"` composition is verified end-to-end via `goalzc`'s output later (TProbe's name is private).",
  "new_string": "## ActorManager + RuntimeZC: split catalog from transport (0.5.0-2)\n\nAdds the multi-threaded zerocopy actor runtime, split across two\nmodules with disjoint responsibilities:\n\n- `actor_manager.rs` — actor model surface. `ActorZC` / `ContextZC` traits + `ActorManager<S>` catalog. Defines *what* an actor is and how the catalog addresses them.\n- `runtime_zc.rs` — message-moving infrastructure. `RuntimeZC<S>` with `run` and `run_no_probe` entry points. One consumer of the actor model surface above; future transports (M:N scheduler, alternative channels) would be peers.\n\nThe shipped shape deviates from the `0.5.0-0` plan in four ways,\nall decided during review:\n\n- Two types instead of one — `ActorManager<S>` (catalog) + `RuntimeZC<S>` (transport). The runtime moves messages; the manager holds instances and assigns ids; they only touch through `take_actors` + `probe_name_prefix` when `run` is called.\n- Two files instead of one — actor model surface lives in `actor_manager.rs`, transport in `runtime_zc.rs`. Reinforces the type-level split at the file level so future growth on either side (control-plane manager-actors; alternative transports) lands cleanly.\n- Direct instance registration rather than factory closures. `ActorZC: Send` is on the trait, so the factory pattern's \"don't require Send\" benefit is moot; `mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 1 })` reads cleaner than `mgr.add_actor(|| PingPong { peer: 1 })`.\n- Initial messages live in the app, not the runtime. `run(&mut mgr, initial_messages, …)` takes them as a parameter; neither manager nor runtime stores them. The benchmark / app is what knows \"ping-pong needs one seed message\".\n\n### Tests added (6 new total, 28 lib tests)\n\nIn `actor_manager::tests`:\n\n- `take_actors_drains_and_resets` — after `take_actors`, the manager is empty and follow-up `add` restarts ids from 0.\n- `manager_exposes_probe_name_prefix` — verifies the prefix accessor; full `\"<prefix>.actor<id>\"` composition is verified end-to-end via `goalzc`'s output later (TProbe's name is private).\n\nIn `runtime_zc::tests`:\n\n- `ping_pong_runs_and_shuts_down` — two-actor ping-pong drives traffic for 40 ms each phase; both actors record nonzero counts.\n- `pool_is_full_after_shutdown` — after `run` returns, every buffer that was ever in flight has come back; `pool.free_len() == pool.size()`.\n- `run_no_probe_returns_counts` — probe-clean path runs the same workload, returns counts only, drains the pool the same way.\n- `runtime_pool_accessor_returns_same_handle` — `RuntimeZC::pool()` exposes the caller's handle (verifies `msg_size` / `size`)."
}

16	- Explore `linkme` / `inventory` for a benchmark harness on top of
17	  `tprobe` — self-registering benchmarks (no `main()` edits per
18	  benchmark); possibly reusable for actor registration too [[9]]
19	- When a benchmark harness lands (criterion and/or the
20	  linkme/inventory idea at `[9]`), add a CPU-warmup helper to
21	  `tprobe` — probably `tprobe::warmup::cpu_boost(iterations)` or
22	  `cpu_boost(Duration)` — since `calibrate()` no longer warms
23	  the CPU itself. Harnesses that call `calibrate()` directly
24	  (without an app-level dispatch loop) need to prime the CPU to
25	  boost frequency first
26	- Add `--repeat N` flag to goal1/goal2 to run the full
27	  warmup+measurement sequence N times and report cross-run mean
28	  and stdev on `mean min-p99` / `adj mean min-p99`. Built-in
29	  replacement for the `for _ in 1..=30; do goal1 2 ...` shell
30	  loop; gives the empirical repeatability number A/B work
31	  depends on. See [[18]] for floors, technique, and
32	  expected-scale heuristics
33	- Revisit band-table `range` semantics — drop the `+1` so
34	  `first == last → range = 0` (true "no spread" instead of
35	  today's "1 tk / rounds to 0 ns"). `last - first` reads
36	  more naturally and matches what a reader expects "range"
37	  to mean. Pre-existing behavior, not introduced in 0.3.0-3
38	- **Bound `TProbe`'s records-buffer memory.** Each scope
39	  appends a 32-byte `Record` to an unbounded `Vec`; at
40	  `goal1 --inner 10` (~13M scopes/s), 20 s of measurement
41	  buffers 260M records ≈ 8 GB minimum, ~16 GB after
42	  `Vec` power-of-2 over-allocation, which swaps/OOMs on
43	  typical dev boxes. Pre-existing from 0.1.0-2 when the
44	  probe was vendored; becomes hard to miss at long
45	  measurement windows + small `inner`. Options (pick one
46	  once we instrument drain cost):
47	  - (a) **Hard cap + truncation counter.** Fixed max
48	    records (e.g. 64M ≈ 2 GB); past the cap, increment a
49	    dropped counter and skip the `push`. Report shows
50	    `truncated: N scopes dropped`. Preserves hot-path
51	    cost. Partial data on very long runs.
52	  - (b) **Periodic inline drain.** When records hit a
53	    threshold, drain into the histogram and continue.
54	    Memory bounded. Adds occasional drain stalls (ms-ish)
55	    that show up in the `p99-max` tail.
56	  - (c) **Inline drain, no buffer.** `end` / `end_batch`
57	    compute `per_event` and call `hist.record` directly.
58	    Fully bounded memory, no stalls, but adds ~15–20 ns
59	    to the hot path — non-trivial when the probe is
60	    measuring ~5 ns work per event.
61	  - (d) **Skinnier record (just `u64` per-event ticks).**
62	    4× memory reduction. Buys time but doesn't fix the
63	    unbounded-growth shape.
64	- Clean up `SingleThreadRuntime::run_for` inner loop:
65	  (a) remove the unnecessary `let Self { actors, queue, probe } = self;`
66	      destructure — disjoint `&mut` through named field paths
67	      (`self.actors[i]`, `self.queue`, `self.probe`) compiles
68	      without the split;
69	  (b) replace `while done < inner { …; done += 1; }` with
70	      `for _ in 0..inner { …; done += 1; }` (break on empty
71	      queue stays) — more idiomatic for a known upper bound,
72	      same generated code under `--release`;
73	  (c) update the struct doc comment that references
74	      "field-split-borrows actors and queue".

```
  - Lets the app build initial messages from the same pool the runtime uses.
  - Lets tests inspect `free_len` after shutdown to verify drain.
  - Lets `goalzc-crit` keep the pool across `iter_custom` calls (long-lived) while the runtime is rebuilt each call.
- `Send` bound on `ActorZC` (not `ContextZC`).
  - Actors get moved into spawned threads; must be `Send`.
  - `ContextZC` lives only inside one thread (constructed per-recv inside `actor_loop`); no `Send` needed.
- `let _ = sender.send(...)` in `MultiCtxZC::send` — closed channel drops the message silently.
  - The `Err(SendError(SignalZC::User(msg)))` case drops the `SignalZC`, which drops the inner `PooledMsg`, which returns the buffer to the pool — no leak.
- Drain assertion as part of the test contract.
  - `pool_is_full_after_shutdown` codifies "every PooledMsg that was ever in flight returns to the pool by the time `run` joins".
  - Non-obvious because it depends on `Drop for PooledMsg`, mpsc receivers dropping queued messages on close, and main-thread senders being dropped before join.
  - Codifying it now means a future change that breaks one of those steps gets caught immediately.

### Future direction (noted, not built today)

- `ActorManager` itself becomes an `ActorZC` for control-plane work — a sibling actor on the same Runtime that handles "register a new actor at runtime", "lookup by name", "drain probes", etc.
- Multiple manager-actors for separate concerns (GUI, CLI, log drain, probe collect) all running on one Runtime.
- Discovery beyond `u32` ids — name-based, type-indexed.
- Closure-based init for richer post-spawn / pre-warmup work (`init: FnOnce(&Mailboxes<S>)`) if a workload ever needs more than a static `initial_messages` list.
- `add_with_factory` escape hatch if a `!Send`-during-construction actor type ever appears.
```

The 0.5.0-0 \"Shape\"\nsubsection above is the *original plan* — `0.5.0-1` and\n`0.5.0-2` deviated from it (see those sections), so this\nhand-off is the canonical starting point for the next\nsession rather than the plan-marker shape.\n\n### Status\n\n- `0.5.0-0` — plan marker. Done & committed.\n- `0.5.0-1` — `Pool` + `PooledMsg` + `BufRefStore` + `MutexLifo`. Done & committed.\n- `0.5.0-2` — `actor_manager.rs` (catalog + traits) + `runtime_zc.rs` (transport). Done; awaiting commit at hand-off time.\n- `0.5.0-3` — `bin/goalzc.rs`. Pending.\n- `0.5.0-4` — `benches/goalzc-crit.rs`. Pending.\n- `0.5.0` — closing marker. Pending.\n\n### As-built API surface (deviations from `0.5.0-0` baked in)\n\n- `Pool::new(msg_size: u32, msg_count: u32) -> Pool<MutexLifo>` — pre-allocates `msg_count` `Box<[u8]>` of `msg_size` bytes; no lazy growth, no `MAX_SIZE` const.\n- `pool.get_msg(size: usize) -> Result<PooledMsg<S>, PoolError>` where `PoolError ∈ { SizeTooLarge { requested, max }, NoMsgs }`.\n- `pool.msg_size() -> u32`, `pool.size() -> usize` — use `msg_size()` for `--size` validation; `NoMsgs` is the saturation signal at runtime.\n- `crates/actor-x1/src/actor_manager.rs`:\n  - `trait ActorZC<S = MutexLifo>: Send` — `fn handle_message(&mut self, ctx: &mut dyn ContextZC<S>, msg: &[u8])`.\n  - `trait ContextZC<S = MutexLifo>` — `get_msg(size) -> Result<PooledMsg<S>, PoolError>`, `send(dst_id: u32, msg: PooledMsg<S>)`.\n  - `ActorManager::new(prefix: &str)` / `add<A: ActorZC<S> + 'static>(actor: A) -> u32` / `take_actors() -> Vec<…>` / `probe_name_prefix() -> &str`.\n- `crates/actor-x1/src/runtime_zc.rs`:\n  - `RuntimeZC::new(pool: Pool<S>) -> Self` / `pool() -> &Pool<S>`.\n  - `run(&mut self, mgr, initial_messages: Vec<(u32, PooledMsg<S>)>, warmup, measurement, pin_cores: &[usize]) -> Vec<(u64, TProbe)>`.\n  - `run_no_probe(...) -> Vec<u64>` — same orchestration, probe-free hot loop.\n- App pattern (drives both `goalzc` and `goalzc-crit`):\n  ```rust\n  let pool = Pool::new(size, 4);                    // 4 buffers ≫ ping-pong steady state of 2\n  let mut rt  = RuntimeZC::new(pool.clone());\n  let mut mgr = ActorManager::new(\"goalzc.dispatch\");\n  let a = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 1 });\n  let _ = mgr.add(PingPong { peer: 0 });\n  let initial = vec![(a, pool.get_msg(size).expect(\"seed\"))];\n  let results = rt.run(&mut mgr, initial, warmup, measurement, &pins);\n  ```\n\n### 0.5.0-3 — `bin/goalzc.rs`\n\n- CLI mirrors `goal2`'s positional / flags: `duration_s` (positional), `--warmup`, `--ticks`, `--decimals`, `--pin`.\n- New flag: `--size N` (`u32`, default 64). Validate `1 ≤ N ≤ pool.msg_size()` at startup; error cleanly if violated. The pool is constructed at runtime, so this is a runtime check, not a const.\n- Pool sizing: 4 buffers covers ping-pong (steady state is 2). A `--pool-count` flag is a future tweak, not needed for 0.5.0.\n- Use `RuntimeZC::run` (probe-instrumented).\n- Output: throughput summary (use `tprobe::fmt::commafmt` or similar) + per-actor band tables. Crib pattern from `crates/actor-x1/src/bin/goal2.rs`.\n- Doc comments on `#[arg]` fields with bullet lists need `#[arg(verbatim_doc_comment, ...)]` per the CLAUDE.md clap caveat.\n- Smoke-test post-install: `goalzc 1 --size 64`, `goalzc 1 --size 64 --pin 0,1`.\n\n### 0.5.0-4 — `benches/goalzc-crit.rs`\n\n- Pattern: `iter_custom`, fresh runtime per sample, scaled by `measurement * iters / total_count`. Lift directly from `crates/actor-x1/benches/goal2-crit.rs`.\n- **Use `RuntimeZC::run_no_probe`, not `run`.** The lesson from `goal2-crit`'s probe contamination is the entire reason `run_no_probe` exists; calling `run` here would re-introduce the same bug.\n- Add `[[bench]]` entry to `crates/actor-x1/Cargo.toml`:\n  ```\n  [[bench]]\n  name = \"goalzc-crit\"\n  harness = false\n  ```\n- Default to `--size 64`. Sweeping sizes is optional — `Throughput::Bytes(size as u64)` if size-sweeping; `Throughput::Elements(1)` for fixed-size.\n- Cross-check: run `goalzc` and `goalzc-crit` at the same size; throughput should agree within the goal2-vs-goal2-crit window (~5–10%; `goal2`/`goal2-crit` reported 228 K vs 240 K msg/s during 0.4.0 development).\n\n### 0.5.0 — close\n\n- Bump `crates/actor-x1/Cargo.toml` from `0.5.0-N` → `0.5.0`.\n- Update `crates/actor-x1/README.md`:\n  - Binaries section: add `goalzc` (with `--size` description).\n  - Benches section: add `goalzc-crit`.\n- `notes/todo.md`: move the In-Progress `[20]` entry to `## Done` with reference `[20]`.\n- Final `vc-x1 push main` to land the closing commit.\n\n### Gotchas / non-obvious things\n\n- **Drain invariant**: `pool.free_len() == pool.size()` must hold after every `run` / `run_no_probe` returns. Tests `pool_is_full_after_shutdown` and `run_no_probe_returns_counts` codify this; don't break them.\n- **Probe contamination**: never call `RuntimeZC::run` from a criterion bench. Use `run_no_probe`.\n- **`--size` validation** hits `pool.msg_size()`, not a const. The bound is set when the pool is built; the CLI pool gets built from the `--size` flag, so the validation is \"is `--size` ≤ `pool.msg_size()` after pool construction\" — easiest is `Pool::new(size, 4)` then nothing further to validate, since by construction `pool.msg_size() == size`. The validation matters more when sizes are runtime-variable (future multi-size sub-pools).\n- **Bullet doc comments on `#[arg]` fields → reflowed to prose by clap unless `verbatim_doc_comment` is set.** See `vc-x1/src/init.rs` for worked examples; the CLAUDE.md \"Writing style: prefer sub-bullets\" section calls this out.\n- **Initial messages are the app's responsibility** — neither manager nor runtime stores them. `goalzc` / `goalzc-crit` build the seed `PooledMsg` from the same `pool` they pass to `RuntimeZC::new`.\n- **`PingPong` test struct**: lives in `runtime_zc::tests`. `goalzc` / `goalzc-crit` will need their own copy (it's small) or a shared `pub` version under `crate::actors` — leaving as a duplication choice for the next session."
}

runtime_zc.rs holds the message-moving infrastructure
(RuntimeZC with run / run_no_probe). Also reorgs the 0.5.0
ladder into its own chores file.

- crates/actor-x1/src/runtime_zc.rs: new — RuntimeZC transport, SignalZC / MultiCtxZC / actor_loop_* internals, run + run_no_probe (4 tests).
